<script>
        // Micro-interaction for Restore buttons
        document.querySelectorAll('button[title="Restore Asset"]').forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                const card = e.target.closest('.archive-card');
                card.style.opacity = '0.5';
                card.style.transform = 'translateY(-10px) scale(0.95)';
                card.style.transition = 'all 0.4s ease-out';
                
                setTimeout(() => {
                    card.remove();
                    // In a real app, toast notification or sync logic here
                }, 400);
            });
        });

        // Search highlight effect
        const searchInput = document.querySelector('input[type="text"]');
        searchInput.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
            const query = e.target.value.toLowerCase();
            document.querySelectorAll('.archive-card h3').forEach(title => {
                const card = title.closest('.archive-card');
                if (title.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(query)) {
                    card.style.display = 'flex';
                } else {
                    card.style.display = 'none';
                }
            });
        });
    </script>

<script>
        // Micro-interactions for form feedback
        const form = document.getElementById('asset-form');
        form.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            const btn = e.target.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
            const originalContent = btn.innerHTML;
            
            // Visual feedback for submission
            btn.disabled = true;
            btn.classList.add('opacity-80');
            btn.innerHTML = `
                <span class="animate-spin material-symbols-outlined">sync</span>
                Registering...
            `;
            
            setTimeout(() => {
                btn.innerHTML = `
                    <span class="material-symbols-outlined">check_circle</span>
                    Asset Registered
                `;
                btn.classList.remove('bg-primary');
                btn.classList.add('bg-tertiary-container', 'text-on-tertiary-container');
                
                // Reset after 2 seconds
                setTimeout(() => {
                    btn.disabled = false;
                    btn.classList.remove('opacity-80', 'bg-tertiary-container', 'text-on-tertiary-container');
                    btn.classList.add('bg-primary');
                    btn.innerHTML = originalContent;
                    form.reset();
                    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
                }, 2000);
            }, 1500);
        });

        // Hover effect for photo upload
        const photoContainer = document.querySelector('section .relative');
        const photoInput = photoContainer.querySelector('input');
        
        photoInput.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
            if (e.target.files && e.target.files[0]) {
                const reader = new FileReader();
                reader.onload = function(event) {
                    photoContainer.style.backgroundImage = `url('${event.target.result}')`;
                    photoContainer.style.backgroundSize = 'cover';
                    photoContainer.style.backgroundPosition = 'center';
                    photoContainer.querySelector('.pointer-events-none').style.display = 'none';
                    
                    // Add a "change" overlay
                    const overlay = document.createElement('div');
                    overlay.className = "absolute bottom-4 right-4 bg-black/50 text-white p-2 rounded-full backdrop-blur-md";
                    overlay.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined">edit</span>';
                    photoContainer.appendChild(overlay);
                };
                reader.readAsDataURL(e.target.files[0]);
            }
        });
    </script>
</body></html>

<script>
        // Simple Interaction logic
        const confirmBtn = document.getElementById('confirm-btn');
        const successToast = document.getElementById('success-toast');

        confirmBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            // Visual feedback
            confirmBtn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined animate-spin">refresh</span> Processing...';
            confirmBtn.classList.add('opacity-80');
            
            setTimeout(() => {
                confirmBtn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined">check_circle</span> Asset Checked Out';
                confirmBtn.classList.remove('bg-primary');
                confirmBtn.classList.add('bg-tertiary');
                
                // Show toast
                successToast.classList.remove('-translate-y-32');
                successToast.classList.add('translate-y-0');

                setTimeout(() => {
                    successToast.classList.remove('translate-y-0');
                    successToast.classList.add('-translate-y-32');
                    
                    // Reset button after 3 seconds
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        confirmBtn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined" style="font-variation-settings: \'FILL\' 1;">check_circle</span> Confirm Check-Out';
                        confirmBtn.classList.add('bg-primary');
                        confirmBtn.classList.remove('bg-tertiary');
                        confirmBtn.classList.remove('opacity-80');
                    }, 2000);
                }, 3000);
            }, 1200);
        });

        // Input Focus Effects
        const inputs = document.querySelectorAll('input, select, textarea');
        inputs.forEach(input => {
            input.addEventListener('focus', () => {
                input.parentElement.classList.add('ring-offset-2');
            });
            input.addEventListener('blur', () => {
                input.parentElement.classList.remove('ring-offset-2');
            });
        });
    </script>
</body></html>

<script>
        // Micro-interaction for the scanner frame: random "lock-on" glitches
        const scanFrame = document.querySelector('.border-2');
        setInterval(() => {
            if (Math.random() > 0.9) {
                scanFrame.classList.add('scale-[1.02]', 'brightness-125');
                setTimeout(() => {
                    scanFrame.classList.remove('scale-[1.02]', 'brightness-125');
                }, 100);
            }
        }, 3000);

        // Flashlight toggle simulation
        const flashBtn = document.querySelector('button:has(.material-symbols-outlined:contains("flashlight_on"))');
        let flashOn = false;
        if (flashBtn) {
            flashBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                flashOn = !flashOn;
                const icon = flashBtn.querySelector('.material-symbols-outlined');
                icon.textContent = flashOn ? 'flashlight_off' : 'flashlight_on';
                icon.classList.toggle('filled-icon', flashOn);
                flashBtn.classList.toggle('bg-primary', flashOn);
                flashBtn.classList.toggle('text-white', flashOn);
            });
        }

        // Custom selector helper for icons
        document.querySelectorAll('.material-symbols-outlined').forEach(icon => {
            if(icon.textContent === 'qr_code_scanner') {
                icon.style.fontVariationSettings = "'FILL' 1";
            }
        });
    </script>

        // Simple Interaction logic for filters
        const filterButtons = document.querySelectorAll('button[class*="rounded-full"]');
        filterButtons.forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                // Clear active states (simplistic implementation)
                filterButtons.forEach(b => {
                    b.classList.remove('bg-primary', 'text-on-primary');
                    b.classList.add('bg-surface-container-high', 'text-on-surface-variant');
                });
                // Set active state
                btn.classList.add('bg-primary', 'text-on-primary');
                btn.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-high', 'text-on-surface-variant');
            });
        });
    </script>

<!-- Success Feedback Overlay (Hidden by default, used for interaction) -->
<div class="fixed bottom-24 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 bg-inverse-surface text-inverse-on-surface px-6 py-3 rounded-full font-label-lg flex items-center gap-2 opacity-0 transition-opacity duration-300 pointer-events-none" id="copyFeedback">

<script>
        // Micro-interactions
        const copyBtn = document.querySelector('button .material-symbols-outlined:contains("content_copy")')?.parentElement;
        if(copyBtn) {
            copyBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                const toast = document.getElementById('copyFeedback');
                toast.classList.remove('opacity-0');
                toast.classList.add('opacity-100');
                setTimeout(() => {
                    toast.classList.remove('opacity-100');
                    toast.classList.add('opacity-0');
                }, 2000);
            });
        }

        // Handle ripple or press effect on primary buttons
        document.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener('touchstart', () => btn.classList.add('opacity-80'));
            btn.addEventListener('touchend', () => btn.classList.remove('opacity-80'));
        });
    </script>

<!-- Subtle Background Glow Interaction (Vanilla JS) -->
<div class="fixed pointer-events-none w-64 h-64 bg-primary/5 rounded-full blur-3xl -z-10 transition-opacity duration-300 opacity-0" id="glow-cursor"></div>
<script>
        document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
            const glow = document.getElementById('glow-cursor');
            glow.style.opacity = '1';
            glow.style.left = `${e.clientX - 128}px`;
            glow.style.top = `${e.clientY - 128}px`;
        });

        document.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
            document.getElementById('glow-cursor').style.opacity = '0';
        });

        // Search feedback
        const searchInput = document.querySelector('input[type="text"]');
        searchInput.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
            const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.bg-surface-container-lowest');
            const query = e.target.value.toLowerCase();
            
            cards.forEach(card => {
                const title = card.querySelector('h3').textContent.toLowerCase();
                if (title.includes(query)) {
                    card.style.display = 'flex';
                    card.classList.add('animate-in', 'fade-in', 'slide-in-from-bottom-2');
                } else {
                    card.style.display = 'none';
                }
            });
        });
    </script>

        document.querySelectorAll('.timeline-item').forEach(item => {
            item.addEventListener('click', () => {
                const card = item.querySelector('.glass-card');
                card.classList.add('scale-[0.98]', 'bg-surface-container-low');

<script>
        // Simple micro-interaction for Save Changes button
        const saveBtn = document.querySelector('button.bg-primary-container.text-on-primary');
        saveBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            const originalContent = saveBtn.innerHTML;
            saveBtn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined animate-spin">sync</span> Updating...';
            saveBtn.classList.add('opacity-80');
            saveBtn.disabled = true;

            setTimeout(() => {
                saveBtn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined">check_circle</span> Changes Saved';
                saveBtn.classList.remove('bg-primary-container');
                saveBtn.classList.add('bg-tertiary-container');
                
                setTimeout(() => {
                    saveBtn.innerHTML = originalContent;
                    saveBtn.classList.remove('bg-tertiary-container', 'opacity-80');
                    saveBtn.classList.add('bg-primary-container');
                    saveBtn.disabled = false;
                }, 2000);
            }, 1200);
        });

        // Toggle focus state visual simulation (not needed with browser focus, but adding for flair)
        const inputs = document.querySelectorAll('input');
        inputs.forEach(input => {
            input.addEventListener('focus', () => {
                input.parentElement.classList.add('ring-2', 'ring-primary', 'ring-inset');
            });
            input.addEventListener('blur', () => {
                input.parentElement.classList.remove('ring-2', 'ring-primary', 'ring-inset');
            });
        });
    </script>

<script>
        // Simple Micro-interaction for search input clear
        const input = document.querySelector('input');
        const clearBtn = input.nextElementSibling;
        
        clearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            input.value = '';
            input.focus();
        });

        // Tab selection visual feedback
        const filterChips = document.querySelectorAll('.flex.gap-2.mt-4 button');
        filterChips.forEach(chip => {
            chip.addEventListener('click', () => {
                filterChips.forEach(c => {
                    c.classList.remove('bg-primary-container', 'text-on-primary-container');
                    c.classList.add('bg-surface-container-high', 'text-on-surface-variant');
                });
                chip.classList.add('bg-primary-container', 'text-on-primary-container');
                chip.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-high', 'text-on-surface-variant');
            });
        });
    </script>

<script>
        // Micro-interaction for condition chips
        document.querySelectorAll('input[name="condition"]').forEach(radio => {
            radio.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
                // Add a subtle haptic feedback simulation
                if (window.navigator.vibrate) {
                    window.navigator.vibrate(10);
                }
            });
        });

        // Simple button click ripple effect or visual state feedback
        const mainButton = document.querySelector('button.bg-primary');
        mainButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
            mainButton.classList.add('opacity-90');
            mainButton.innerHTML = `<span class="material-symbols-outlined animate-spin">sync</span> Processing...`;
            setTimeout(() => {
                mainButton.innerHTML = `<span class="material-symbols-outlined">check_circle</span> Success`;
                mainButton.classList.remove('bg-primary');
                mainButton.classList.add('bg-green-600');
            }, 1500);
        });
    </script>

        // Simple micro-interaction for feedback
        document.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                if(!btn.innerText.includes('Print')) {
                    const originalText = btn.innerHTML;

                    btn.disabled = true;
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        btn.innerHTML = originalText;
                        btn.disabled = false;
                    }, 1200);

        // Simple search interaction for demo
        const searchInput = document.querySelector('input');
        searchInput.addEventListener('focus', () => {
            searchInput.parentElement.classList.add('scale-[1.02]');
        });
        searchInput.addEventListener('blur', () => {
            searchInput.parentElement.classList.remove('scale-[1.02]');
        });

        // Tactile feedback for cards
        const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.location-card');
        cards.forEach(card => {
            card.addEventListener('click', function() {
                this.classList.add('bg-surface-container');
                setTimeout(() => {
                    this.classList.remove('bg-surface-container');
                }, 150);
            });
        });
    </script>

<!-- Interactive Layer -->
<script>
        let currentSlide = 0;
        const totalSlides = 3;
        const carousel = document.getElementById('carousel');
        const dots = document.querySelectorAll('#carousel + div > div');

        function slideCarousel(direction) {
            currentSlide = (currentSlide + direction + totalSlides) % totalSlides;
            carousel.style.transform = `translateX(-${currentSlide * 100}%)`;
            
            // Update dots
            dots.forEach((dot, index) => {
                dot.style.opacity = index === currentSlide ? '1' : '0.5';
            });
        }

        // Auto-slide occasionally
        setInterval(() => slideCarousel(1), 8000);

        // Simple Tab switching logic
        const tabs = document.querySelectorAll('nav ul button');
        tabs.forEach(tab => {
            tab.addEventListener('click', () => {
                tabs.forEach(t => {
                    t.classList.remove('text-primary', 'font-bold', 'tab-active');
                    t.classList.add('text-on-surface-variant');
                });
                tab.classList.remove('text-on-surface-variant');
                tab.classList.add('text-primary', 'font-bold', 'tab-active');
            });
        });
    </script>

<script>
        // Password visibility toggle logic
        const toggleBtn = document.getElementById('togglePassword');
        const passwordInput = document.getElementById('password');
        
        toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            const isPassword = passwordInput.type === 'password';
            passwordInput.type = isPassword ? 'text' : 'password';
            toggleBtn.querySelector('span').innerText = isPassword ? 'visibility_off' : 'visibility';
        });

        // Form Submission Interaction
        const form = document.querySelector('form');
        const overlay = document.getElementById('successOverlay');
        const successCard = document.getElementById('successCard');

        form.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            
            // Show Success Animation
            overlay.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
            overlay.classList.add('opacity-100');
            successCard.classList.remove('scale-90');
            successCard.classList.add('scale-100');

            // Simulate Redirect
            setTimeout(() => {
                window.location.href = '#dashboard';
            }, 1800);
        });

        // Focus ring enhancement
        const inputs = document.querySelectorAll('input');
        inputs.forEach(input => {
            input.addEventListener('focus', () => {
                input.parentElement.querySelector('.material-symbols-outlined').style.color = '#004ac6';
            });
            input.addEventListener('blur', () => {
                input.parentElement.querySelector('.material-symbols-outlined').style.color = '#737686';
            });
        });
    </script>
</body></html>

        // Micro-interaction for cards
        document.querySelectorAll('div[class*="transition-transform"]').forEach(card => {
            card.addEventListener('mousedown', () => {
                card.style.transform = 'scale(0.98)';
                card.style.boxShadow = '0px 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)';
            });
            card.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
                card.style.transform = 'scale(1.02)';
                card.style.boxShadow = '0px 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)';
            });
        });
    </script>

<script>
        // Simple micro-interaction for tab switching
        document.querySelectorAll('nav[class*="overflow-x-auto"] button').forEach(button => {
            button.addEventListener('click', () => {
                // Reset others
                document.querySelectorAll('nav[class*="overflow-x-auto"] button').forEach(b => {
                    b.className = 'px-6 py-2 rounded-full text-label-lg font-label-lg bg-surface-container-highest text-on-surface-variant hover:bg-surface-variant whitespace-nowrap';
                });
                // Set active
                button.className = 'px-6 py-2 rounded-full text-label-lg font-label-lg bg-primary text-on-primary shadow-md whitespace-nowrap';
            });
        });

        // Add press effect to cards
        document.querySelectorAll('.asset-card').forEach(card => {
            card.addEventListener('mousedown', () => {
                card.style.boxShadow = '0px 2px 4px rgba(0,0,0,0.1)';
            });
            card.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
                card.style.boxShadow = '0px 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)';
            });
        });
    </script>

<script>
        // Simple logic to simulate completion or transition
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            const progressBar = document.querySelector('.progress-bar-fill');
            const statusLabel = document.querySelector('.text-label-lg');
            
            const statuses = [
                "Connecting to Cloud",
                "Syncing Local Assets",
                "Optimizing Scanner",
                "Ready"
            ];

            let index = 0;
            const statusInterval = setInterval(() => {
                if (index < statuses.length) {
                    statusLabel.textContent = statuses[index];
                    index++;
                } else {
                    clearInterval(statusInterval);
                    // In a real app, this would trigger navigation to Dashboard
                }
            }, 800);

            // Subtle interaction: subtle move of logo on mouse move
            document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
                const moveX = (e.clientX - window.innerWidth / 2) / 100;
                const moveY = (e.clientY - window.innerHeight / 2) / 100;
                const logo = document.querySelector('.animate-reveal-logo');
                if (logo) {
                    logo.style.transform = `translate(${moveX}px, ${moveY}px)`;
                }
            });
        });
    </script>
</body></html>
